 425: @dataclass
 426: class Slide:
 427:     layout_id: str
 428:     title: str
 429:     blocks: list[Block] = field(default_factory=list)
 430:     id: str | None = None
 431:     notes: str | None = None
 432: 
 433:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 434:         out: dict[str, Any] = {"layout_id": self.layout_id, "title": self.title,
 435:                                "blocks": [b.to_dict() for b in self.blocks]}
 436:         if self.id:
 437:             out["id"] = self.id
 438:         if self.notes:
 439:             out["notes"] = self.notes
 440:         return out
 441: 
 442: 
 443: @dataclass
 444: class DeckMeta:
 445:     title: str
 446:     subtitle: str | None = None
 447:     language: str = "mixed"
 448:     source: str | None = None
 449: 
 450:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 451:         out: dict[str, Any] = {"title": self.title, "language": self.language}
 452:         if self.subtitle:
 453:             out["subtitle"] = self.subtitle
 454:         if self.source:
 455:             out["source"] = self.source
 456:         return out
 457: 
 458: 
 459: @dataclass
 460: class Deck:
 461:     deck: DeckMeta
 462:     slides: list[Slide] = field(default_factory=list)
 463:     ir_version: str = IR_VERSION
 464: 
 465:     # -- parsing [REDACTED]
 466: 
 467:     @classmethod
 468:     def from_dict(cls, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> Deck:
 469:         """Structural parse. Does NOT check ``layout_id`` or budgets -- those need the
 470:         manifest, so they live in :meth:`validate_against_manifest` and in fit.py."""
 471:         version = raw.get("ir_version")
 472:         if version != IR_VERSION:
 473:             raise IRValidationError("ir_version",
 474:                                     f"expected {IR_VERSION!r}, got {version!r}")
 475:         d = raw.get("deck") or {}
 476:         if not d.get("title"):
 477:             raise IRValidationError("deck.title", "required")
 478:         meta = DeckMeta(title=d["title"], subtitle=d.get("subtitle"),
 479:                         language=d.get("language", "mixed"), source=d.get("source"))
 480: 
 481:         slides = []
 482:         for i, s in enumerate(raw.get("slides", [])):
 483:             path = f"slides[{i}]"
 484:             if not s.get("layout_id"):
 485:                 raise IRValidationError(f"{path}.layout_id", "required")
 486:             if not s.get("title"):
 487:                 raise IRValidationError(f"{path}.title", "required")
 488:             blocks = [_parse_block(b, f"{path}.blocks[{j}]")
 489:                       for j, b in enumerate(s.get("blocks", []))]
 490:             slides.append(Slide(layout_id=s["layout_id"], title=s["title"], blocks=blocks,
 491:                                 id=s.get("id"), notes=s.get("notes")))
 492:         if not slides:
 493:             raise IRValidationError("slides", "a deck needs at least one slide")
 494:         return cls(deck=meta, slides=slides, ir_version=version)
 495: 
 496:     @classmethod
 497:     def from_json(cls, text: str) -> Deck:
 498:         return cls.from_dict(json.loads(text))
 499: 
 500:     @classmethod
 501:     def load(cls, path: Path) -> Deck:
 502:         return cls.from_json(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 503: 
 504:     # -- serialising [REDACTED]
 505: 
 506:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 507:         return {"ir_version": self.ir_version,
 508:                 "deck": self.deck.to_dict(),
 509:                 "slides": [s.to_dict() for s in self.slides]}
 510: 
 511:     def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
 512:         return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=indent, ensure_ascii=False)
 513: 
 514:     def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
 515:         Path(path).write_text(self.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")
 516: 
 517:     # -- validation [REDACTED]
 518: 
 519:     def validate_against_manifest(self, manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
 520:         """Cross-check every ``layout_id`` against the real template.
 521: 
 522:         Returns a list of human-readable errors (empty == valid). Returning rather than
 523:         raising is deliberate: the AI-adapter's retry prompt wants *all* the problems at
 524:         once, not just the first one.
 525:         """
 526:         known: set[str] = set()
 527:         for master in manifest.get("masters", []):
 528:             for lay in master.get("layouts", []):
 529:                 known.add(lay["layout_id"])
 530: 
 531:         errors: list[str] = []
 532:         for i, slide in enumerate(self.slides):
 533:             if slide.layout_id not in known:
 534:                 nearest = sorted(known)[:5]
 535:                 errors.append(
 536:                     f"slides[{i}].layout_id: {slide.layout_id!r} is not a layout in this "
 537:                     f"template. Valid ids include: {', '.join(repr(k) for k in nearest)}"
 538:                 )
 539:         return errors
 540: 
 541: 
 542: def load_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
 543:     """The normative JSON Schema, for the model's tool-use definition and for jsonschema."""

--- PHASE CHANGE DETAIL 109-170 ---
 109:   2. `_parse_and_validate_slide(slide_dict, manifest)` -- wraps the slide in a throwaway
 110:      one-slide deck and reuses `Deck.from_dict` (structural errors JSON Schema cannot
 111:      express, e.g. a ragged table row) + `Deck.validate_against_manifest` (unknown
 112:      `layout_id`, belt-and-suspenders with step 1).
 113:   3. `_check_slide_budget(parsed, layout)` -- the critic's second and third/fourth fixes,
 114:      implemented **locally**, without calling `render.fit.check_budget` (still
 115:      `NotImplementedError`): checks `slide.title` against a title-role placeholder's char
 116:      cap, and each `slide.blocks[i]` against `body_placeholders[i]`'s cap, positionally
 117:      (per `ir/schema.json`'s own documented "blocks map onto non-title placeholders in
 118:      order"). See the module docstring on `_check_slide_budget` for the title-placeholder
 119:      fallback heuristic and why it's needed for this specific manifest.
 120: 
 121:   On any of the two exception types, the error string becomes the next `repair_note` and
 122:   the loop retries; after `max_repairs` failed attempts it raises `RuntimeError` naming the
 123:   slide id, its layout, and the last error. Returns a `GenerationResult` with the assembled
 124:   `Deck` (title truncated to 120 chars, the schema's cap, since `topic` is unbounded
 125:   caller-supplied text), `attempts` = total `fill_slide` calls across the whole deck,
 126:   `repaired` = True if any slide needed more than one attempt, and `token_usage={}`
 127:   (neither CLI track exposes token accounting, so this is honestly empty, not a stub).
 128: 
 129: ## Design choice: aggregate vs. positional body-budget check
 130: 
 131: Chose positional (`blocks[i]` vs `body_placeholders[i]`) over summing all body text
 132: against a combined pool, since `ir/schema.json` documents the positional mapping as the
 133: actual renderer contract, and a combined-pool check can miss a single overflowing block
 134: whose overage is masked by another under-using block's slack. Blocks beyond the number of
 135: available body placeholders are not checked (the schema says they fall through to
 136: free-floating shapes with no declared budget) -- not a gap, since there's nothing to check
 137: them against.
 138: 
 139: ## Environment quirk (same as previously logged for this task)
 140: 
 141: This staging clone (`stg.pbZbUGS6`) contains only `tasks/AI_Powered_PPT/src/` under this
 142: project -- no `prompts/`, `reference/`, or `tasks/phase3_integration/` exist here (the
 143: repo-root `.gitignore` has `tasks/*` with only `.gitkeep` tracked, so none of this
 144: project's data files are ever committed into a fresh clone). `prompts/00_system_deck_author.md`,
 145: `prompts/10_outline_storyboard.md`, `prompts/20_slide_fill.md`, and
 146: `reference/layout_manifest.json` / `reference/layout_catalog.md` were all read from the
 147: OUTER tree to verify their placeholder tokens and manifest shape against the code written
 148: here. The code resolves them relative to its own file location at runtime
 149: (`Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]`), correct in any full checkout (verified: those
 150: files exist at that relative depth in the outer tree) but not exercisable end-to-end from
 151: inside this stage, since the stage lacks `prompts/`/`reference/` entirely.
 152:

VERDICT: BLOCK

`_check_slide_budget()` only compares each block’s total character count to `max_bullets * max_chars_per_bullet`. It misses per-item line rounding: e.g., four 11-character bullets in a 6×10-character placeholder total 44 and pass, but each wraps to two lines (8 lines total), so it overflows. This bypasses the repair loop despite the manifest’s “a bullet that wraps consumes another slot” rule.
